- **glab-mr-assign**: Add / remove MR reviewers and assignees WITHOUT the two footguns — (a) verify usernames exist via `glab api "users?search=…"` before calling `glab mr update`, and (b) use `+username` / `-username` semantics so a `--reviewer` call ADDS instead of REPLACING the existing list. Trigger on "set X as reviewer", "add reviewer", "assign the MR to me", "reassign MR", "remove reviewer", "who can review this". Never accept first-name guesses ("brice", "sofiane") without resolving to a real username first. ([Dauliac/dotfiles](https://github.com/Dauliac/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/modules/home-manager/ai/capability/skills/engineering/glab-mr-assign) · ★2)
- **glab-mr-automerge**: Arm Merge-When-Pipeline-Succeeds (MWPS / auto-merge) on a GitLab MR. Atomic — takes an MR IID (or the MR associated with the current branch), calls `glab mr merge --when-pipeline-succeeds` with the project's merge strategy, then verifies `merge_when_pipeline_succeeds=true` came back green. Refuses to arm on a still-draft MR unless `--keep-draft` is passed (draft MRs never merge, so arming MWPS is a silent no-op).
